# Amazon SageMaker HyperPod Recipe Containers

This document shows container images available by AWS region for each recipe,
organized by model family and JumpStart model ID.

Container images are shown for both Kubernetes (K8S) and SageMaker Jobs (SM) pipelines. When both pipelines use the same container, only one URI is shown.

## Table of Contents

- [Llama](#llama)
- [Qwen](#qwen)
- [DeepSeek](#deepseek)
- [GPT-OSS](#gpt-oss)
- [Nova](#nova)
- [Gemma](#gemma)
- [Ministral](#ministral)
- [Nemotron](#nemotron)
